### LP best response details

```
def lp_best_response(a, timeout=1.0):
    n = a.size
    A = _build_A(a)
    # vars g[0:n], t
    c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    A_ub = np.hstack((A, -np.ones((2*n-1,1))))
    b_ub = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    b_eq = np.ones(1)
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)] # t≥0
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':timeout, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9,'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
    if success: g=res.x[:n]; g=clip; sum maybe 1; if sum>0 normalize; return g
```
This solves min cross. However t may be less than? Good.
But direct min t may be infeasible? Always feasible g=a, t=C. So yes.

def lp_direction_search(a, stop_time, max_iter, quiet):

    best=a; best_M=conv_max(best)

def soft_max_obj(y, beta, base):
   a = softmax(y)
   c = conv(a)
   z = beta*(c-base)
   zmax = z.max()
   logZ = zmax + log(sum(exp(z-zmax)))
   obj = 1/beta * (logZ + zmax? Wait logsumexp = zmax+logsum; obj=(1/β) logsumexp(β(c-base)))
   p = exp(z - logZ)  # since logZ = log sum exp(β(c-base))
   h = grad_c_weighted(p, a)
   grad_y = a * (h - np.dot(a,h))
   return obj, grad_y
```

Need compute weighted gradient h correctly:
```
def grad_weighted(p, a):
    n = len(a)
    # p length 2n-1
    # cross[j]=sum_i a_i p[i+j]
    # Use sliding_window_view if available
    from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view
    w = sliding_window_view(p, n)  # shape (n,n); w[j,i]=p[i+j]
    cross = w @ a
    p_diag = np.zeros(n)
    idx2 = 2*np.arange(n)
    mask = idx2 < 2*n-1
    p_diag[mask] = a[mask] * p[idx2[mask]]
    return cross + p_diag
```
Check p_diag: cross includes a_j p_{j+j}=a_j p_{2j}; need add another a_j p_{2j}. Good.
But sliding_window_view for p length 2n-1, window n => n rows. For n large (e.g., 1500), view size 2.25M, memory 18MB, okay; matrix-vector uses BLAS maybe. For n=191 fine. If trying n up to 1024, view 1M, 8MB. Good. But for very large 5000, 50M 400MB avoid. We'll restrict.

Alternatively compute cross via FFT for speed: cross_j = (a * p_rev?) Let's derive for fallback. But sliding window fine.

def linprog_direction(a, stop_time, max_iters, rel_cap=0.5, abs_cap=None):
   best = a, best_M
   a = normalize
   it=0
   while time<stop and it<max:
      c=conv(a); M=c.max()
      if M<best: update
      # If M close to average? maybe break if t small.
      d = _linearized_direction(a, c, M, rel_cap)
      if d is None or t >= -tol: break/stuck
      # line search over alpha
      cand = a + alpha d; clip positive? If any negative, restrict alpha_max.
      find best alpha using vector c? We can compute conv(a, cand) maybe direct. Since n small, just conv each alpha? Or use quadratic with c_a, c_d? Candidate a+αd: conv = c_a + 2α c_ad + α^2 c_d. Need ensure coefficient nonnegative? d can negative, c_d may have negative? conv of d with d can negative? d not nonnegative, so c_d can negative; but α small. Use linear combo.
      precompute c_a, c_ad, c_dd. For α grid, m = max(c_a + 2α c_ad + α^2 c_dd). Since d sum0, c sum? no matter.
      Choose α that minimizes m; if m < best_M*(1-1e-12), accept α=best_alpha.
      a = a + α d; normalize (sum may be exactly1 but do)
      it++
```

Need time allocation. Linearized LP is iterative; each iteration maybe expensive if many calls. We'll run it as a function `_direction_polish(start, stop_time, max_iters, tune_caps)`. It returns best a,M. It can take initial caps list and adapt.

Pseudo:
```
def _linstep_polish(a0, stop_time, max_iters=5000):
    a=normalize(a0)
    best=a; best_M=conv(a).max()
    rel_caps = [0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
    cap_idx=0
    stuck=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time>stop_time-0.2: break
        c=conv(a); M=c.max()
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-13): best_M=M; best=a.copy(); stuck=0
        else: stuck+=1
        if stuck > 30:
            if cap_idx < len(rel_caps)-1: cap_idx+=1
            else:
               # random small perturbation
               a = normalize(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,n)))
               stuck=0
               continue
        rel = rel_caps[cap_idx]
        d,t = _linearized_direction(a,c,M, rel, abs_cap=max(1e-4, 0.1*rel?))
        if d is None or t > -1e-10:
            # try smaller rel? maybe if cap_idx not last, cap_idx++; else perturb/break
            cap_idx = min(cap_idx+1, len-1)
            if cap_idx == len-1 and stuck>20: maybe random
            continue
        # line search
        alpha,m = _line_search_dir(a,d,c,M,t)
        if alpha>0 and m < best_M:
            a = normalize(a+alpha*d)
        else:
            # shrink cap? maybe half, or break
            if cap_idx < len-1: cap_idx+=1
            else: break
    return best, best_M
```

Setting rel_cap=1,bounds lower=-a_i upper=a_i allows full grid. Then if no improvement, truly no linear direction within full simplex. Feasible. So caps sequence maybe start 0.1 and increase? For aggressive improvement, use larger caps initially. If no improvement, can try even larger but not beyond 1. Let's use rel_cap candidates [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]? Start moderate to avoid overshoot; line search handles. If no improvement, increase to 1 (full). If t positive? t min maybe negative if any direction. ### Line search utilities
`_line_search_candidates(c_a, c_new_or_quad, M_current, alpha_max=1.0)`.
For candidate b normalized: c_t = (1-α)^2 c_a + 2α(1-α) c_ab + α^2 c_b. We can write a generic for quadratic coefficients:
```
def _quad_line_search(c_base, c_cross, c_target, M_base, alpha_max=1.0):
    # value(α) = c_base + linear? For a to b: (1-α)^2 c_a + 2α(1-α)c_ab + α^2 c_b
    # = c_a + 2α(c_ab-c_a) + α^2(c_b -2c_ab + c_a)
```
For direction d (a+αd): value = c_a + 2α c_ad + α^2 c_dd.
We can implement both by passing arrays base, lin, quad: value = base + α lin + α^2 quad.
For a→b: base=c_a; lin=2(c_ab-c_a); quad=c_b-2c_ab+c_a.
For a+d: base=c_a; lin=2 c_ad; quad=c_dd.

Find minimum max over α in (0,α_max]. We can do grid 200 points? Vector: for α shape (K,1), V = base[None]+... shape (K,m). For n=191, m=381, K=200 ->76k, fine. But doing each iteration maybe 1000 ->76M, okay. Use K=101. Then refine around best with 50 points. Return α with min m. Also ensure candidate nonnegative: For b nonnegative, any α okay. For d, positivity restricts α_max. Use α_max.

Numerical: c_a etc from np.convolve; small negative due to FFT? For d negative, combo could be negative; use max with0? But maximum of negative not issue; if all negative? Not. Use `np.maximum(v,0.0)` before max? The actual candidate nonnegative, combos should nonnegative if α within positivity; due to roundoff small negative. Use `v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)`. But if d has negative causing some coefficients should be positive, roundoff small; okay.

### `_direction_polish` design in detail
```
def _direction_polish(a0, stop_time, rng, max_iters=10000, prefer='auto'):
    a = normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n>800: # use simpler? 
    best_a=a; best_M=...
    t0=...; it=0; no_imp=0
    caps = [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]
    cap_idx=0
    use_br_every = 3
    while time<stop_time-0.1 and it<max_iters:
        c = _conv(a); M=c.max()
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-12): best update no_imp=0
        else no_imp+=1
        improved=False
        # Directional LP (linearized) if cap_idx maybe
        if n <= 700:
           d,t = _linearized_direction(a,c,M, caps[cap_idx], timeout=0.4)
           if d is not None and t < -1e-10:
              cand, m = _line_search_dir(a,d,c,timeout safe)
              if cand is not None and m < M*(1-1e-10):
                  a=cand; improved=True
        if not improved and (it % use_br_every == 0 or no_imp>5):
           g = _lp_best_response(a, timeout=0.6)
           if g is not None:
              cand,m = _line_search_ab(a,g)
              if cand is not None and m < M*(1-1e-10):
                  a=cand; improved=True
        if improved:
           it_no_fail=0
        else:
           it_no_fail+=1
           # adjust cap or perturb
           if it_no_fail < 5:
               cap_idx = min(cap_idx+1, len(caps)-1)
           elif it_no_fail < 15:
               # small random perturb
               z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
               z += rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)
               a = softmax(z); 
               no_imp=0
               it_no_fail=0
           else:
               # more random, if still stuck break to BFGS
               # maybe try reverse or sym? 
               break
        it++
    return best_a,best_M
```

### Resampling
```
def _resample(a, m):
    a=normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    x0=np.linspace(0,1,n+1); y0=np.concatenate(([0],np.cumsum(a)))
    # piecewise linear mass interpolation: We want b such that integral piecewise? Simple: cumulative at midpoints.
    x_mid0=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    x_mid1=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    b=np.interp(x_mid1, x_mid0, a) # approximates density
    b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); return b/s if s>0 else ones
```
Better use cumulative linear: interpolate cumulative at fractional indices to get segments. But midpoint density okay. For mass preservation, normalize.

Could use `np.resize`? no.

### Building A efficiently
```
def _build_A(a):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # vectorized: for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=a
    for j, aj in enumerate(a):
        A[j:j+n, j] = aj
    return A
```
Actually assigning scalar to slice. Loop n. Could use `np.add.at`? Fine.

For gradient G:
```
def _build_G(a):
    A=_build_A(a); G=A.copy()
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    idx=2*np.arange(n)
    valid=idx<m
    G[idx[valid], np.arange(n)[valid]] += a[valid]
    return G
```

def _conv(a,b=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); if b is None: b=a
   if max(a.size,b.size) <= 1024:
       return np.convolve(a,b)
   fsize=next_fast_len(a.size+b.size-1)
   fa=rfft(a,fsize); fb=rfft(b,fsize)
   c=irfft(fa*fb,fsize)[:a.size+b.size-1]
   if b is a: c = 0.5? rfft real? irFFT returns real; no rounding? Need maybe `c *=?` For rfft, inverse uses 1/fsize; product correct. For real, no half issue. Return c.
```

### Softmax gradient sliding window
Need import `sliding_window_view` maybe not available? It is in numpy 1.20+. Use fallback loop if ImportError.
```
def _weighted_grad(p,a):
    n=a.size
    try:
        view=sliding_window_view(p,n)
        cross=view @ a
    except Exception:
        cross=np.empty(n)
        for j in range(n): cross[j]=np.dot(a,p[j:j+n])
    dg=np.zeros(n)
    idx=2*np.arange(n); valid=idx<2*n-1
    dg[valid] += a[valid]*p[idx[valid]]
    return cross+dg
```
Wait `cross` includes a_j p_{2j}; adding another a_j p_{2j}. Good.

If p has NaNs? no.

### BFGS objective exact
```
def _bfgs_run(a, s, stop_time, maxiter=200):
    n=a.size
    c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
    base=M
    beta=max(10.0, s/max(M,1e-12))
    y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15)); y-=y.mean()
    # maybe random tiny perturb
    def fun(y):
        zc=softmax(y)
        cc=conv(zc)
        z=beta*(cc-base)
        zm=z.max()
        ez=np.exp(z-zm)
        logZ=zm+np.log(ez.sum())
        obj=logZ/beta
        p=ez/ez.sum()
        h=_weighted_grad(p,zc)
        grad=zc*(h - np.dot(zc,h))
        return obj, grad
    res=minimize(fun, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=[(-50,50)]*n, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':40,'disp':False})
    an=softmax(res.x)
    return normalize(an), _score_norm(an)
```
However `fun` returns tuple; minimize accepts. But time: It will call fun many times; no time check. maxiter limit. If n large and maxiter too high, may run long. Use `maxiter` based on n: `maxiter = min(150, max(30, 3000//n * 50?))`. For n=191, 150. For n=64, 150. Good. We can also set `maxfun` perhaps 3000. Options `maxfun` works. Use `maxfun=max(500, 2000)`.

If `res.status` not 0 but x available, use. Evaluate.

Add random perturbation before BFGS: `a_pert = normalize(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)))` with sigma 0.01-0.05. But if perturb worsens, BFGS may not recover. We can run with original and perturbed. To manage, in `_bfgs_refine` loop:
```
for cycle in range(max_cycles):
  for s in s_vals:
     a_try = a if cycle==0 else perturb
     run; if score<best: update
```
But if a_try perturbed from best, BFGS objective starts worse; it might return worse exact; we only accept if better than best. Good.

    from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view

```

Helpers:
```
_EPS=1e-12

def _get_parent():
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if h is not None:
           p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       p=None
    if p is None:
       p=np.ones(191,dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    if p.size==0 or not np.isfinite(p).all() or p.sum()<0.01:
       p=np.ones(191,dtype=np.float64)
    return p
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(a.size if a.size else 1)/max(1,a.size)
   return a/s
```

`_soft_max`:
```
def _softmax(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float); ym=y-max(y); e=np.exp(y-ym); s=e.sum(); if s==0 or not finite: e=ones; return e/e.sum()
```

`_conv`:
```
def _conv(a,b=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if b is None: b=a
   b=np.asarray(b,float)
   if a.size==0 or b.size==0: return np.array([0.0])
   if a.size+b.size-1 <= 2048: # threshold for FFT maybe >2048? np.convolve O(4M) okay; but many BFGS if n=1024 ->2M, maybe okay? Use 2048 length
       c=np.convolve(a,b)
   else:
       fsize=next_fast_len(a.size+b.size-1)
       c=irfft(rfft(a,fsize)*rfft(b,fsize), fsize)[:a.size+b.size-1]
   if b is a:
       # if nonnegative? Could have d? b is a only for self, a may nonnegative. clip small neg
       np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
   return c
```
For `_conv(a,d)` b is not a, don't clip.
Threshold: For n=2048, conv length4095, np.convolve 16M multiplications maybe 0.1s; if many BFGS too slow. Use FFT for len>2048. FFT for n=191? rfft faster but overhead; np.convolve likely faster. Threshold 1024 maybe. Use `if max(a.size,b.size)<=768:` np else FFT? Let's set 768. For n=1024, length2047 >768, FFT. But we likely not BFGS n>512. For LP convolution line search use a,b nonnegative length≤512, np.convolve fast. Use threshold 1024.

`_score_norm(a)` assumes sum1:
```
def _score_norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return inf
   # If not sum1, compute exact formula
   c=_conv(a)
   mb=float(c.max())
   return float(2.0*n*mb/(s*s))
```
This is exact for any sum. Use for internal.

`_score_seq`:
```
def _score_seq(seq):
   try: return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
   except: return _score_norm(np.asarray(seq,float))
```

`_update_best` can be inline.

### build A
```
def _build_A(a):
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((m,n))
   # Use loop
   for j in range(n):
       A[j:j+n,j]=a[j]
   return A
```
Could vectorize: `np.take`? Loop okay. For many calls maybe overhead. We can optimize using `for j, val in enumerate(a): A[j:j+n, j] = val`. Good.

`_build_G` copies.

### LP solve helper
```
def _lp_solve(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq=None,b_eq=None,bounds=None, timeout=1.0, stop=None):
   if stop is not None:
      rem=stop-time.monotonic()
      tl=min(timeout, rem-0.2)
      if tl<0.05: return None
   else: tl=timeout
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':tl, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
   except Exception: return None
   if res.status==0 and res.x is not None: return np.asarray(res.x,float)
   return None
```
HiGHS time limit if reaches may have status 10? ignore.

Primary LP:
```
def _lp_best_response(a, M, A=None, timeout=0.8, stop=None):
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1
   if A is None: A=_build_A(a)
   b_ub=np.full(m, M + 1e-10)
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   x=_lp_solve(c_obj,A,b_ub,None,None,bounds,timeout,stop)
   if x is None: return None
   g=np.clip(x,0,None); s=g.sum();
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   return g/s
```

Tie LP:
```
def _lp_tie_break(a,M,A,w,timeout=0.6,stop=None):
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1
   if A is None: A=_build_A(a)
   b_ub=np.full(m,M+1e-9)
   c_obj=-np.asarray(w,float)
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,n)); A_eq[0,:]=1
   b_eq=np.array([1.0])
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   x=_lp_solve(c_obj,A,b_ub,A_eq,b_eq,bounds,timeout,stop)
   if x is None: return None
   g=np.clip(x,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s<=1e-12: return None
   return g/s
```
Equality sum ensures feasible; `b_eq` 1. M maybe current max; a is feasible. If numerical, slab.

But if M small and w constant, LP objective no effect; still returns some feasible maybe a? Could return a due to warm? We can check if g close to a; if so, add random perturbation to w. Not crucial.

Linearized direction:
```
def _linearized_direction(a,c,M,G,timeout=0.6,stop=None,rel_cap=0.5):
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1
   r=np.maximum(M-c,0)+1e-10
   # bounds
   u=np.minimum(rel_cap*a, 0.5)  # if a sum1, 0.5 huge
   # if a_i very small, allow small increase
   low=np.where(a>1e-12, -u, 0.0)
   up=np.where(a>1e-12, np.maximum(u, 1e-8), 1e-8)
   # if rel_cap>=1, low=-a? u=a? cap maybe 0.5. For sum1, a max can be <0.5, okay. If need full, rel_cap=1 u=a.
   c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
   A_ub=np.empty((m,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=G; A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
   b_eq=np.array([0.0])
   bounds=list(zip(low,up))+[(-1.0,1.0)]
   x=_lp_solve(c_obj,A_ub,r,A_eq,b_eq,bounds,timeout,stop)
   if x is None: return None,None
   d=x[:n]; t=x[-1]
   if not np.isfinite(d).all(): return None,None
   d -= d.mean()
   if t < -1e-9:
       return d,t
   return None,None
```
Need G built. If G not built, build. Use rel_cap sequence. For full full simplex, rel_cap=1 -> low=-a, up=a. If a_i=0, low0, up floor. Good.

But bounds `low=-u` where u=rel_cap*a. If rel_cap=1, low=-a, but if a_i=0 low0. `up=a` plus floor. Good.

Maybe `u=np.minimum(rel_cap*a, None)` no cap. Since sum1, max a maybe 0.5; okay. Use no abs cap.

If `t` very negative but d large, line search. Good.

### Line search quad
```
def _line_search_quad(base, lin, quad, M_ref, alpha_max, make_cand):
    if alpha_max <= 1e-8: return None,None
    alpha_max = min(alpha_max, 1.0) * (1-1e-12)
    # initial alphas
    as1 = np.linspace(0, alpha_max, 101)
    # add geomspace if alpha_max > 1e-3
    if alpha_max > 1e-3:
        as2 = np.geomspace(1e-6, alpha_max, 100)
        alphas = np.unique(np.concatenate((as1, as2)))
    else:
        alphas = as1
    def evalu(alphas):
        aa=alphas[:,None]
        V=base[None,:] + aa*lin[None,:] + aa*aa*quad[None,:]
        V=np.maximum(V,0.0)
        return V.max(axis=1)
    m=evalu(alphas); i=int(np.argmin(m))
    # refine around i
    if 0<i<len(alphas)-1:
       lo=alphas[max(0,i-3)]; hi=alphas[min(len(alphas)-1,i+3)]
    else:
       lo=0.0; hi=alpha_max
    al_ref=np.linspace(lo,hi,80)
    m=evalu(al_ref); i=int(np.argmin(m))
    alpha=float(al_ref[i]); mc=float(m[i])
    if alpha <= 1e-10 or not np.isfinite(alpha): return None,None
    cand=make_cand(alpha)
    if cand is None or not np.isfinite(cand).all(): return None,None
    cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); s=cand.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return None,None
    cand=cand/s
    c_new=_conv(cand); m_exact=float(c_new.max())
    if np.isfinite(m_exact) and m_exact < M_ref:
       return cand, m_exact
    # fallback: if approximate much lower but exact not, maybe try smaller alpha? But grid should.
    return None,None
```
This verify uses `M_ref` current M (not best). If m_exact lower but due to normalization? Good.

For a→b:
```
def _line_search_ab(a,b, M_ref):
    c_a=_conv(a); c_b=_conv(b); c_ab=_conv(a,b)
    base=c_a; lin=2*(c_ab-c_a); quad=c_b-2*c_ab+c_a
    def make(alpha): return (1-alpha)*a+alpha*b
    return _line_search_quad(base,lin,quad,M_ref,1.0,make)
```
Compute c_a each time? We already have c. Pass c_a. Signature:
```
def _line_search_ab(a,b,c_a,M_ref):
   c_b=_conv(b); c_ab=_conv(a,b); ...
```
For d:
```
def _line_search_dir(a,d,c_a,M_ref):
   neg=d<0; if neg.any(): amax = np.min(-a[neg]/(-d[neg])); else amax=1.0
   # also require a+αd positive; if amax >1? d bounds sum? use min(amax,1)
   c_ad=_conv(a,d); c_dd=_conv(d,d)
   base=c_a; lin=2*c_ad; quad=c_dd
   def make(alpha): return a+alpha*d
   return _line_search_quad(base,lin,quad,M_ref,amax,make)
```
If d has negative and alpha_max very small, line search may fail. Good.

Convolutions for line search: `_conv(a,b)` for b normalized nonnegative; `_conv(d,d)` for d negative. Use length thresholds.

Potential issue: `np.unique` of concatenated geomspace and linspace length ~200; evaluating V as 200x381 okay. Could be 400x1000 for n=512 okay.

### `_direction_polish` detailed code
```
def _direction_polish(a0, stop_time, rng, max_iters=10000):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
    if n==0: return a,inf
    c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max()); best=a.copy(); best_M=M
    caps=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]
    cap_idx=0
    fail=0
    it=0
    tie_used=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 0.3 and it < max_iters:
        c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
        if M < best_M*(1-1e-12): best_M=M; best=a.copy(); fail=0
        else: fail+=1
        if fail > 60: break
        A=_build_A(a)
        G=A.copy()
        idx=2*np.arange(n); valid=idx<2*n-1
        G[idx[valid], np.arange(n)[valid]] += a[valid]
        improved=False
        # primary best response
        g=_lp_best_response(a,M,A,timeout=0.7,stop=stop_time)
        if g is not None:
            cand,m_new=_line_search_ab(a,g,c,M)
            if cand is not None:
               a=cand; improved=True
        if not improved:
            # maybe linearized direction if cap_idx < len
            d,t=_linearized_direction(a,c,M,G,timeout=0.7,stop=stop_time,rel_cap=caps[cap_idx])
            if d is not None:
               cand,m_new=_line_search_dir(a,d,c,M)
               if cand is not None:
                  a=cand; improved=True
        if not improved:
            # tie break candidates
            tried=False
            if tie_used < 3:
               # linear direction
               w=np.linspace(1.0,0.0,n)
               g=_lp_tie_break(a,M,A,w,timeout=0.5,stop=stop_time)
               if g is not None:
                   cand,_=_line_search_ab(a,g,c,M)
                   if cand is not None: a=cand; improved=True; tried=True
            if not improved and tie_used < 3:
               w=rng.normal(size=n)
               g=_lp_tie_break(a,M,A,w,timeout=0.5,stop=stop_time)
               if g is not None:
                   cand,_=_line_search_ab(a,g,c,M)
                   if cand is not None: a=cand; improved=True
            if not improved:
               # small perturbation
               sigma=0.05 if fail<20 else 0.1
               y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15)); y += rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
               a=_softmax(y)
               cap_idx=(cap_idx+1)%len(caps)
               tie_used += 1 if not improved else 0? 
               if fail>50: break
        if improved:
            fail=0; tie_used=0
            # if improved a lot? could keep cap same
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail % 5 ==0: cap_idx=min(cap_idx+1,len(caps)-1)
        it +=1
    return best, best_M
```
Need update best after each improved? We update at top of loop. If final a improved but no next loop due time, best might miss. At end compute final score and update.
```
   fin_M=float(_conv(a).max()); if fin_M<best_M: best=a; best_M=fin_M
```
Also after perturbation no improvement; top next updates.

But `fail` increments at top if no improvement from previous; then if improved, set 0; else fail++ duplicate. Let's streamline:
```
prev_M = M? 
```
Simpler:
At loop start compute M; if M < best_M: best update.
Try methods; if any improved, `a` changed. Next iteration will update if better. For stuck count, use `no_update` that increments if a not changed this iteration.
```
no_update=0; failed_tie=0
...
changed=False
...
if changed: no_update=0; cap_idx=0? maybe
else:
   no_update+=1
   if no_update>...: perturb; changed=True? perturbation itself maybe not improve but allow next
```
Perturbation changes a but best may not. If after perturb line search not, next loop.
Use `no_update > 30` break.

### BFGS refine clean
```
def _bfgs_refine(a0, stop_time, rng, max_cycles=2, s_values=None):
   a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
   c=_conv(a); best_M=float(c.max()); best=a.copy()
   if s_values is None: s_values=[1,3,10,30,80]
   for cycle in range(max_cycles):
      for s in s_values:
         if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 4: break
         a_try = a
         if cycle>0:
            sigma = 0.03 / (cycle+1)
            a_try = _normalize(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)))
         # allocate stop for single: min(stop_time, now + 20?) 
         # To allow multiple, set per_time = min(30, remaining/len)
         a_new = _bfgs_single(a_try, s, stop_time, maxiter=80 if n>300 else 150)
         if a_new is not None:
            M=float(_conv(a_new).max())
            if M < best_M*(1-1e-12):
               best_M=M; best=a_new; a=a_new
      if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 4: break
   return best,best_M
```
But if no improvement in a cycle, next cycle perturb smale. Good.

`_bfgs_single` should not exceed stop. We can compute `maxiter` based on remaining but no time callback. For n=191, 150 iter maybe <1s? Need safe. Could be 2s. Fine. If stop soon, maxiter may overshoot. Use `maxiter = min(30, max(10, int((stop_time-now)//0.2)))`? But if each iter 0.01s, too low. Since no exact, approximate: for n<=200, maxiter=100; for 400 maxiter=50. A single call of 100 iter likely <1s. To be safe, set `maxiter=50` and `maxfun=800`. We can call multiple s. Use `stop` not inside. After call check time. If it overruns, not ideal but okay if total budget with reserve. Need avoid one call >1300. 50 iter with line search maybe <5s. Good.

Maybe use `maxiter=80, maxfun=1200`. Options.

`_bfgs_single` code:
```
def _bfgs_single(a,s,stop_time,maxiter=80):
   if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 1: return None
   n=a.size; c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0: return a
   base=M; beta=max(10.0, s/max(M,1e-12))
   y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15)); y-=y.mean()
   bounds=[(-60,60)]*n
   def fun(y):
      aa=_softmax(y)
      cc=_conv(aa)
      z=beta*(cc-base)
      zm=float(z.max())
      ez=np.exp(z-zm)
      Z=ez.sum()
      if Z<=0 or not np.isfinite(Z): return 1e3, np.zeros(n)
      logZ=zm+math.log(Z)
      obj=logZ/beta
      p=ez/Z
      h=_weighted_grad(p,aa)
      grad=aa*(h - float(aa.dot(h)))
      if not np.isfinite(grad).all(): grad=np.zeros(n)
      return obj, grad
   try:
      res=minimize(fun,y,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':50,'maxfun':2000,'disp':False})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res.x is None: return a
   an=_normalize(_softmax(res.x))
   return an
```
Need `math` imported. If `minimize` overruns stop, still okay? But we should bound maxfun. For n=191, 2000 fun maybe 2s. Good. For 10 s-values, 20s.

If beta huge and base current, objective near 0; `fun` returns negative if cc<base? At start some equal0, logZ=0, obj=0. Good.

Maybe add small random perturb to y for s high if gradient zero:
```
if cycle? Not in single.
```

### Coarse search
```
def _coarse_search(base, N_target, stop_time, rng):
   out=[]
   now=time.monotonic()
   ns=[32,64]
   for n in ns:
      if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 10: break
      # time per n
      per_n=(stop_time-now)/2? 
      # generate inits
      inits=_make_inits(base,n,rng,k=6)
      per_init=max(2.0,(stop_time-time.monotonic())/len(inits))
      for init in inits:
         if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 5: break
         alloc=min(time.monotonic()+per_init, stop_time-2)
         try:
            a_opt,_=_direction_polish(init, alloc, rng, max_iters=500)
            a_target=_resample(a_opt,N_target)
            out.append(a_target)
         except Exception: pass
   # maybe top
   scored=[]
   for a in out:
      if len(a)==0: continue
      sc=_score_norm(a)
      if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
   scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
   return [a for sc,a in scored[:2]]
```
`_direction_polish` on coarse alloc maybe few seconds. Use `max_iters` lower maybe 300.

`_make_inits` needs parameter `k` for random. Let's implement.

### Resample and inits
```
def _resample(a,m):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   if n==m: return a.copy()
   if n<2:
      return np.full(m, a[0]/m if a.size else 1/m)
   x0=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x1=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   b=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
   b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(m)/m
   return b/s

def _make_inits(base,n,rng,k=6):
   inits=[]
   # base
   b=_resample(base,n)
   inits.append(b)
   inits.append(_normalize(b+b[::-1]))
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   for aexp in (0.3,0.5,0.75):
       v=(x+1e-6)**(-aexp); inits.append(_normalize(v))
       v=(1-x+1e-6)**(-aexp); inits.append(_normalize(v))
   for bexp in (0.2,0.4):
       v=(x*(1-x)+1e-5)**(-bexp); inits.append(_normalize(v))
   inits.append(_normalize(np.linspace(0.2,1.0,n)))
   inits.append(_normalize(np.exp(-2.0*np.abs(np.sin(2*np.pi*0.7*x)))))
   for _ in range(k):
       # random smooth positive using Fourier low freq
       passes = max(2, n//10)
       s = np.zeros(n)
       for f in range(1,passes+1):
           amp = 1.0/(f+1)
           phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
           s += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*(np.arange(n)/n)+phase)
       y = 1.0 + 0.5*s
       # blur
       if n>=5:
          kern=np.array([0.1,0.3,0.5,0.3,0.1]); kern/=kern.sum()
          y=np.convolve(y,kern,'same')
       y=np.maximum(y,1e-3); inits.append(_normalize(y))
   # parent noise
   pn=_resample(base,n)
   noisy=_normalize(pn*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25,n)))
   inits.append(noisy)
   return inits
```

def _try_near_lengths(best_a,N,stop_time,rng):
   results=[]
   for n_try in [N-2,N-1,N,N+1,N+2]:
       if n_try<8 or time>stop-10: continue
       init=_resample(best_a,n_try)
       # maybe also invsqrt for new n
       alloc = time.monotonic()+10
       a_,_=_direction_polish(init, alloc, rng, max_iters=500)
       results.append(a_)
   return results
```

### Overall main allocation
Set reserve 30. Let's write concretely:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    if budget < 50:
       parent=_get_parent(); return [float(x) for x in parent]
    deadline=t0+budget-30
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=_get_parent()
    N=parent.size
    best_a=_normalize(parent)
    best_score=_score_norm(best_a)
    # quick inits at N
    inits=_make_inits(parent,N,rng,k=3)
    for a in inits:
       sc=_score_norm(a)
       if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=a
    # coarse if time > 250
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250:
       cs_stop=time.monotonic()+min(120, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.12)
       try:
          coarse=_coarse_search(best_a,N,cs_stop,rng)
          for a in coarse:
             sc=_score_norm(a); update
       except: pass
    # main directional
    main_stop=time.monotonic()+min(350, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.4)
    a,M=_direction_polish(best_a, min(main_stop, deadline-20), rng)
    update
    # bfgs
    b_stop=time.monotonic()+min(250, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.35)
    a,M=_bfgs_refine(best_a, min(b_stop,deadline-20), rng)
    update
    # directional second
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-50:
       d_stop=time.monotonic()+min(180, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.5)
       a,M=_direction_polish(best_a, d_stop, rng)
       update
    # nearby lengths if time >80
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-60:
       l_stop=time.monotonic()+min(80, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.4)
       for n_try in [N-1,N+1,N+2,N-2]:
          if time>l_stop-5: break
          init=_resample(best_a,n_try)
          a_opt,_=_direction_polish(init, min(time.monotonic()+10, l_stop), rng)
          sc=_score_norm(a_opt); update
          # maybe bfgs quick if improved?
    # final bfgs high beta
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-15:
       a,M=_bfgs_refine(best_a, deadline-3, rng, max_cycles=4, s_values=[10,30,80,150])
       update
    # final validation
    final_list=[float(x) for x in np.clip(best_a,0,1000)]
    # normalize? no need but maybe scale to sum=sqrt? Evaluator scale invariant; if sum<=0.01? best sum1, okay.
    try:
       final_score=float(evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except:
       final_score=_score_norm(np.array(final_list))
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
       final_list=[1.0]*32
    # Could rescale values? If list values extremely small? sum1 fine.
    return final_list
```

```

### Let's detail `_direction_polish` code to avoid infinite loops.
Pseudo final:
```
def _direction_polish(a0, stop_time, rng, max_iters=20000):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
    c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max()); best=a.copy(); best_M=M
    caps=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]
    cap_idx=1
    no_update=0; tie_stage=0; it=0
    use_lp = (n <= 700)
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 0.25 and it < max_iters:
        c=_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M): break
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M=M; best=a.copy()
        changed=False
        if use_lp:
            A=_build_A(a)
            G=A.copy()
            idx=2*np.arange(n); valid=idx<2*n-1
            G[idx[valid], np.arange(n)[valid]] += a[valid]
            g=_lp_best_response(a,M,A,timeout=0.6,stop=stop_time)
            if g is not None:
                cand,_=_line_search_ab(a,g,c,M)
                if cand is not None:
                    a=cand; changed=True
            if not changed:
                d,t=_linearized_direction(a,c,M,G,timeout=0.6,stop=stop_time,rel_cap=caps[cap_idx])
                if d is not None:
                    cand,_=_line_search_dir(a,d,c,M)
                    if cand is not None:
                        a=cand; changed=True
        if not changed:
            if use_lp and no_update >= 2:
                # tie candidates
                tried=False
                if tie_stage < 3:
                    if tie_stage % 2 ==0:
                       w=np.linspace(1.0,0.0,n)
                    else:
                       w=rng.normal(size=n)
                    g=_lp_tie_break(a,M,A,w,timeout=0.5,stop=stop_time)
                    if g is not None:
                       cand,_=_line_search_ab(a,g,c,M)
                       if cand is not None:
                          a=cand; changed=True
                if changed: tie_stage=0
                else: tie_stage=(tie_stage+1)%4
            if not changed:
                # base symmetrization
                sym = _normalize(0.5*(a + a[::-1]))
                symM=float(_conv(sym).max())
                if symM < M - 1e-12:
                    a=sym; changed=True
            if not changed:
                # random perturb
                sig = 0.03 + 0.01*(no_update % 5)
                y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15)); y += rng.normal(0,sig,n)
                a=_softmax(y)
                cap_idx = min(cap_idx + 1, len(caps)-1) when no_update? 
```
But if random perturb, not immediately line search; next loop. To avoid continually perturbing without letting line search from new point? Each loop attempts line search first. Good.
Update no_update:
```
        if changed:
            no_update=0
            # optionally reduce cap? keep
        else:
            no_update += 1
            if no_update>50: break
        it +=1
```
But if random perturb changed a, we didn't set changed. We should maybe not count as no_update? Actually if we just perturbed, next loop may find. But if no_update increments immediately after perturb, after 50 perturbations break. We can set `no_update=0` after perturb but also maybe `it_perturb` count. Let's use separate `perturb_count` max 30. If perturbed, reset no_update, increment perturb_count. If no improved for many perturbations break.

perturb_count=0
if not changed:
   ...
   if no_update==? and perturb_count<40:
      perturb; perturb_count+=1; no_update=0; continue

```

def _get_parent():
    p = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = None
    if p is None or p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(p).all():
        p = np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0 or p.sum() < 0.01:
        p = np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64)
    return p
```

`_normalize` if a is list, `a.size`? Use after np.asarray.

```
def _normalize(v):
    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    return a / s
```

`_softmax` similar.

def _score_norm(v):

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

try:
    from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view
except Exception:
    sliding_window_view = None


def _get_parent():
    p = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = None
    if p is None or p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(p).all():
        p = np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0 or p.sum() < 0.01:
        p = np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _normalize(v):
    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    ym = float(y.max())
    e = np.exp(y - ym)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b is None:
        b = a
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = a.size + b.size - 1
    if out_len <= 4096:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    fsize = next_fast_len(out_len)
    c = irfft(rfft(a, fsize) * rfft(b, fsize), fsize)[:out_len]
    return c


def _max_conv(v):
    c = _conv(v)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.maximum(c, 0.0)))


def _score_norm(v):
    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = _max_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def _build_A(a):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a[j]
    return A


def _lp_solve(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq=None, b_eq=None, bounds=None,
              timeout=1.0, stop=None):
    if stop is not None:
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        tl = min(timeout, rem - 0.25)
        if tl < 0.05:
            return None
    else:
        tl = max(0.05, timeout)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={
                "time_limit": tl,
                "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if np.isfinite(x).all():
            return x
    return None


def _lp_best_response(a, M, A=None, timeout=0.7, stop=None):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if A is None:
        A = _build_A(a)
    slack = 1e-9 + 1e-7 * max(0.0, float(M))
    b_ub = np.full(m, float(M) + slack, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    x = _lp_solve(c_obj, A, b_ub, None, None, bounds, timeout, stop)
    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g /= s
    if not np.isfinite(g).all():
        return None
    return g


def _lp_tie_break(a, M, A, w, timeout=0.5, stop=None):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if A is None:
        A = _build_A(a)
    slack = 1e-9 + 1e-7 * max(0.0, float(M))
    b_ub = np.full(m, float(M) + slack, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    x = _lp_solve(c_obj, A, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, timeout, stop)
    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g /= s
    if not np.isfinite(g).all():
        return None
    return g


def _linearized_direction(a, c, M, G, timeout=0.7, stop=None, rel_cap=0.2):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    r = np.maximum(M - c, 0.0) + 1e-10

    u = np.clip(rel_cap * np.maximum(a, 0.0), 0.0, None)
    low = np.where(a > 1e-12, -u, 0.0)
    up = np.where(a > 1e-12, np.maximum(u, 1e-9), 1e-9)

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0
    A_ub = np.empty((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_ub[:, :n] = G
    A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = list(zip(low.tolist(), up.tolist())) + [(-1.0, 1.0)]

    x = _lp_solve(c_obj, A_ub, r, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, timeout, stop)
    if x is None:
        return None, None
    d = x[:n]
    t = float(x[-1])
    d -= float(d.mean())
    if np.isfinite(t) and t < -1e-10:
        return d, t
    return None, None


def _line_search_quad(base, lin, quad, M_ref, alpha_max, make_cand):
    if not np.isfinite(alpha_max) or alpha_max <= 1e-8:
        return None, None
    alpha_max = min(float(alpha_max), 1.0) * (1.0 - 1e-12)
    if alpha_max <= 1e-9:
        return None, None

    al1 = np.linspace(0.0, alpha_max, 101)
    if alpha_max > 1e-6:
        al2 = np.geomspace(1e-8, alpha_max, 100)
        alphas = np.unique(np.concatenate((al1, al2)))
    else:
        alphas = al1

    def evals(als):
        aa = als[:, None]
        V = base[None, :] + aa * lin[None, :] + (aa * aa) * quad[None, :]
        V = np.maximum(V, 0.0)
        return V.max(axis=1)

    vals = evals(alphas)
    i = int(np.argmin(vals))
    lo = alphas[max(0, i - 3)]
    hi = alphas[min(len(alphas) - 1, i + 3)]
    if hi <= lo:
        lo, hi = 0.0, alpha_max
    al_ref = np.linspace(lo, hi, 80)
    vals = evals(al_ref)
    i = int(np.argmin(vals))
    alpha = float(al_ref[i])
    if not np.isfinite(alpha) or alpha <= 1e-10:
        return None, None

    cand = make_cand(alpha)
    if cand is None:
        return None, None
    cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None, None
    cand /= s
    Mc = _max_conv(cand)
    if np.isfinite(Mc) and Mc < M_ref:
        return cand, Mc
    return None, None


def _line_search_ab(a, b, c_a, M_ref):
    c_b = _conv(b)
    c_b = np.maximum(c_b, 0.0)
    c_ab = _conv(a, b)
    c_ab = np.maximum(c_ab, 0.0)
    base = c_a
    lin = 2.0 * (c_ab - c_a)
    quad = c_b - 2.0 * c_ab + c_a

    def make(alpha):
        return (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * b

    return _line_search_quad(base, lin, quad, M_ref, 1.0, make)


def _line_search_dir(a, d, c_a, M_ref):
    neg = d < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        denom = -d[neg]
        num = a[neg]
        bad = denom <= 1e-16
        if np.any(bad):
            return None, None
        amax = float(np.min(num[~bad] / denom[~bad]))
    else:
        amax = 1.0
    amax = min(1.0, amax) * (1.0 - 1e-12)
    if amax <= 1e-9:
        return None, None

    c_ad = _conv(a, d)
    c_dd = _conv(d, d)
    base = c_a
    lin = 2.0 * c_ad
    quad = c_dd

    def make(alpha):
        return a + alpha * d

    return _line_search_quad(base, lin, quad, M_ref, amax, make)


def _direction_polish(a0, stop_time, rng, max_iters=20000):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = _conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    caps = [0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0]
    cap_idx = 1
    no_update = 0
    perturb_count = 0
    tie_stage = 0
    it = 0
    use_lp = (n <= 700 and n >= 2)

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time - 0.25 and it < max_iters:
        c = _conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best = a.copy()

        changed = False
        A = None
        G = None
        if use_lp:
            A = _build_A(a)
            G = A.copy()
            idx2 = 2 * np.arange(n)
            valid = idx2 < (2 * n - 1)
            cols = np.arange(n)
            G[idx2[valid], cols[valid]] += a[valid]

            g = _lp_best_response(a, M, A, timeout=0.65, stop=stop_time)
            if g is not None:
                cand, _ = _line_search_ab(a, g, c, M)
                if cand is not None:
                    a = cand
                    changed = True

            if not changed:
                d, t = _linearized_direction(
                    a, c, M, G,
                    timeout=0.65, stop=stop_time,
                    rel_cap=caps[cap_idx],
                )
                if d is not None:
                    cand, _ = _line_search_dir(a, d, c, M)
                    if cand is not None:
                        a = cand
                        changed = True

        if not changed and use_lp and no_update >= 2:
            if tie_stage < 4:
                if tie_stage % 2 == 0:
                    w = np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n)
                else:
                    w = rng.normal(size=n)
                g = _lp_tie_break(a, M, A, w, timeout=0.45, stop=stop_time)
                if g is not None:
                    cand, _ = _line_search_ab(a, g, c, M)
                    if cand is not None:
                        a = cand
                        changed = True

        if not changed:
            sym = _normalize(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
            symM = _max_conv(sym)
            if symM < M - 1e-12:
                a = sym
                changed = True

        if changed:
            no_update = 0
            tie_stage = 0
            cap_idx = max(0, cap_idx - 1)
        else:
            no_update += 1
            if no_update >= 4 and perturb_count < 60:
                sig = 0.03 + 0.01 * (no_update % 5)
                y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
                y += rng.normal(0.0, sig, n)
                a = _softmax(y)
                no_update = 0
                perturb_count += 1
                cap_idx = min(cap_idx + 1, len(caps) - 1)
                tie_stage += 1
            elif perturb_count >= 60 or no_update > 12:
                break

        it += 1

    c = _conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M_end = float(c.max()) if c.size else float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(M_end) and M_end < best_M:
        best_M = M_end
        best = a.copy()
    return best, best_M


def _weighted_grad(p, a):
    n = a.size
    if sliding_window_view is not None:
        try:
            view = sliding_window_view(p, n)
            cross = view @ a
        except Exception:
            cross = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for j in range(n):
                cross[j] = float(np.dot(a, p[j:j + n]))
    else:
        cross = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            cross[j] = float(np.dot(a, p[j:j + n]))

    out = np.array(cross, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    idx2 = 2 * np.arange(n)
    valid = idx2 < (2 * n - 1)
    out[valid] += a[valid] * p[idx2[valid]]
    return out


def _bfgs_single(a, s, stop_time, maxiter=100):
    if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 0.5:
        return None
    n = a.size
    if n < 1:
        return None
    c = _conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return a

    base = M
    beta = max(10.0, s / max(M, 1e-12))
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
    y -= float(y.mean())
    bounds = [(-60.0, 60.0)] * n

    def fun(yy):
        aa = _softmax(yy)
        cc = _conv(aa)
        cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
        z = beta * (cc - base)
        zm = float(z.max())
        ez = np.exp(z - zm)
        Z = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
            return 1e3, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        logZ = zm + math.log(Z)
        obj = logZ / beta
        p = ez / Z
        h = _weighted_grad(p, aa)
        grad = aa * (h - float(aa.dot(h)))
        if not np.isfinite(grad).all():
            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return obj, grad

    if n <= 220:
        mx = max(40, min(maxiter, 140))
        mf = 2500
    elif n <= 600:
        mx = max(25, min(maxiter, 70))
        mf = 1200
    else:
        mx = max(15, min(maxiter, 35))
        mf = 500

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun, y,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": mx,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 50,
                "maxfun": mf,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res.x is None:
        return a
    an = _normalize(_softmax(res.x))
    if not np.isfinite(an).all():
        return a
    return an


def _bfgs_refine(a0, stop_time, rng, max_cycles=2, s_values=None):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = _conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else float("inf")
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    if s_values is None:
        s_values = [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 80.0]

    for cycle in range(max(1, int(max_cycles))):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 5.0:
            break
        sigma = 0.0
        if cycle > 0:
            sigma = 0.04 / (cycle + 1)
        for s in s_values:
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 4.0:
                break
            a_try = a
            if sigma > 0.0:
                a_try = _normalize(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)))
            ml = 120 if n <= 220 else (60 if n <= 600 else 30)
            an = _bfgs_single(a_try, s, stop_time, maxiter=ml)
            if an is not None:
                Mn = _max_conv(an)
                if np.isfinite(Mn) and Mn < best_M - 1e-14:
                    best_M = Mn
                    best = an.copy()
                    a = an.copy()
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 4.0:
            break
    return best, best_M


def _resample(a, m):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 0:
        return np.full(max(1, m), 1.0 / max(1, m), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(max(1, m), float(a[0]) / max(1, m), dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x1 = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def _make_inits(base, n, rng, k=4):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    inits = []
    b = _resample(base, n)
    inits.append(b)
    inits.append(_normalize(b + b[::-1]))

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    for aexp in (0.3, 0.5, 0.75):
        v = (x + 1e-6) ** (-aexp)
        inits.append(_normalize(v))
        v = (1.0 - x + 1e-6) ** (-aexp)
        inits.append(_normalize(v))

    for bexp in (0.2, 0.45):
        v = (x * (1.0 - x) + 1e-5) ** (-bexp)
        inits.append(_normalize(v))

    inits.append(_normalize(np.linspace(0.15, 1.0, n) ** 2))
    inits.append(_normalize(np.exp(-1.8 * (x - 0.7) ** 2) + 1e-3))

    if n >= 5:
        kern = np.array([0.1, 0.3, 0.4, 0.3, 0.1], dtype=np.float64)
        kern /= kern.sum()
    else:
        kern = None

    for _ in range(max(0, int(k))):
        passes = max(2, n // 12)
        s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in range(1, passes + 1):
            amp = 1.0 / (f + 0.5)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
            s += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + phase)
        y = 1.0 + 0.45 * s
        if kern is not None:
            y = np.convolve(y, kern, mode="same")
        y = np.maximum(y, 1e-3)
        inits.append(_normalize(y))

    for _ in range(max(0, int(k))):
        y = rng.exponential(scale=1.0, size=n) + 1e-4
        if kern is not None:
            y = np.convolve(y, kern, mode="same")
        y = np.maximum(y, 1e-4)
        inits.append(_normalize(y))

    pn = _resample(base, n)
    noisy = _normalize(pn * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)))
    inits.append(noisy)

    return inits


def _coarse_search(base, N_target, stop_time, rng):
    out = []
    now = time.monotonic()
    if stop_time <= now + 5.0:
        return out

    ns = []
    for n in (32, 64, 128):
        if n >= 8 and n <= max(128, int(2 * max(2, N_target))):
            ns.append(n)
    if not ns:
        ns = [max(16, min(64, max(2, N_target)))]

    all_inits = []
    for n in ns:
        for init in _make_inits(base, n, rng, k=3):
            all_inits.append((n, init))

    if not all_inits:
        return out

    total = len(all_inits)
    per = max(0.8, (stop_time - time.monotonic()) / total)

    for _, init in all_inits:
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2.0:
            break
        alloc = min(time.monotonic() + per, stop_time - 1.0)
        try:
            a_opt, _ = _direction_polish(init, alloc, rng, max_iters=5000)
            sc_opt = _score_norm(a_opt)
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt):
                out.append(a_opt)
            target_len = max(2, int(N_target))
            a_res = _resample(a_opt, target_len)
            sc_res = _score_norm(a_res)
            if np.isfinite(sc_res):
                out.append(a_res)
            if time.monotonic() + 0.2 < alloc:
                a_b = _bfgs_single(a_opt, 30.0, alloc, maxiter=50)
                if a_b is not None:
                    sc_b = _score_norm(a_b)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_b):
                        out.append(a_b)
                    a_br = _resample(a_b, target_len)
                    sc_br = _score_norm(a_br)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_br):
                        out.append(a_br)
        except Exception:
            pass

    scored = []
    seen = 0
    for a in out:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        key = (a.size, round(float(_score_norm(a)), 12))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    return [a for _, a in scored[:3]]


def _to_list(a):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all() or a.sum() <= 0.01:
        a = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    if budget < 30.0:
        return _to_list(parent)

    reserve = min(45.0, max(5.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = _normalize(parent)
    best_score = _score_norm(best_a)

    def consider(arr):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        try:
            a = _normalize(arr)
            if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
                return
            sc = _score_norm(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        except Exception:
            return

    N0 = int(parent.size)
    if N0 < 2:
        N0 = 191

    # Lightly screen a few analytically generated shapes at the parent length.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        try:
            inits = _make_inits(parent, N0, rng, k=2)
            for a in inits:
                consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Coarse multi-resolution search; this can find a better global profile.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 200.0:
        cs_stop = time.monotonic() + min(150.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.12)
        try:
            coarse = _coarse_search(best_a, N0, cs_stop, rng)
            for a in coarse:
                consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Main LP/direction polish from the current best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 55.0:
        stop = time.monotonic() + min(450.0, rem * 0.45)
        try:
            a, _ = _direction_polish(best_a, min(stop, deadline - 20.0), rng)
            consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Smoothed-max SLSQP/L-BFGS polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        stop = time.monotonic() + min(280.0, rem * 0.40)
        try:
            a, _ = _bfgs_refine(best_a, min(stop, deadline - 20.0), rng,
                                max_cycles=2, s_values=[1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 80.0])
            consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # A second directional polish from the refined point.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        stop = time.monotonic() + min(220.0, rem * 0.55)
        try:
            a, _ = _direction_polish(best_a, min(stop, deadline - 25.0), rng)
            consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Small-length trials around the best current length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0:
        current_len = int(best_a.size)
        candidates = [current_len - 2, current_len - 1, current_len + 1, current_len + 2]
        candidates = [n for n in candidates if n >= 8 and n <= max(700, int(2 * current_len))]
        # Also try a modestly larger length if it seems cheap enough.
        if current_len <= 300:
            candidates.append(int(round(1.25 * current_len)))
            candidates.append(int(round(1.5 * current_len)))
        candidates = [n for n in candidates if 8 <= n <= 512]
        seen = set()
        trial_stop = time.monotonic() + min(90.0, rem * 0.45)
        for n_try in candidates:
            if n_try in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(n_try)
            if time.monotonic() > trial_stop - 4.0:
                break
            try:
                init = _resample(best_a, n_try)
                alloc = min(time.monotonic() + 12.0, trial_stop - 1.0)
                a_opt, _ = _direction_polish(init, alloc, rng, max_iters=5000)
                consider(a_opt)
                if time.monotonic() + 0.3 < alloc:
                    a_b = _bfgs_single(a_opt, 30.0, alloc, maxiter=50)
                    if a_b is not None:
                        consider(a_b)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final high-sharpness sharpening.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
        try:
            a, _ = _bfgs_refine(best_a, deadline - 6.0, rng,
                                max_cycles=3, s_values=[10.0, 30.0, 80.0, 160.0])
            consider(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_list = _to_list(best_a)
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    final_score = float("inf")
    if ev is not None:
        try:
            final_score = float(ev(final_list))
        except Exception:
            final_score = float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        arr = np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64)
        final_score = _score_norm(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > 10.0:
        final_list = [1.0 / 32.0] * 32

    return final_list
```
